# Yamaha YZ: aftermarket parts research catalog

Research candidates gathered from public web sources (manufacturer pages, dealers, retailers, magazine tests, testers, forums). Each product keeps its source and a confidence: high = the manufacturer lists the part number and fitment; medium = a dealer or major retailer does; unverified = inferred or title-only. Nothing here is verified evidence, nothing is shown to riders yet, and no fitment, price or performance claim should be repeated without checking the source. Performance claims are the maker's or tester's, with their conditions.

Files: bars_clamps.json, bars_clamps2.json, brakes_controls.json, cg-yamaha.json, chains_wheels.json, chassis.json, chassis2.json, cooling_valvetrain.json, dealer-yamaha-genuine.json, drivetrain.json, engine.json, engine2.json, engine_services.json, house_brands.json, house_brands2.json, ms-yamaha.json, p4-bearings-mounts.json, p4-gapfill.json, suspension.json, tuning.json, valvetrain2.json, yamaha-research.json. Built by scripts/aftermarket/build_catalog.py.

Counts: 7695 products, 28204 part numbers, 34 rider/tester findings, 5 project-bike builds, 63 of 63 in-scope bike-years with at least one mapped product.

## Project-bike builds

### [Twisted Development Airbox Mod (2024 Yamaha YZ250F)](https://www.keeferinctesting.com/twisted-development-airbox-mod-2024-yamaha-yz250f/) — Yamaha YZ250F 2024, Keefer Inc Testing 2023-12-31

- intake: Twisted Development Air Boot Kit adapting 2023/24 YZ450F airbox — Restore low-end lost to 2024 airbox; found: Quicker climb through power, more torque feel, 2nd usable in corners; $1,300
- ecu: Vortex ECU mapped by Twisted Development for pump fuel — Support airbox change; found: Race-gas-like feel on pump fuel (Keefer)

### [Top Mods For Your YZ250F](https://pulpmx.com/2026/04/01/top-mods-for-your-yz250f/) — Yamaha YZ250F 2025, PulpMX 2026-04-01

- intake: XPR Motorsports Dual injector kit + Vortex ECU — Bottom-end and rpm response; found: More bottom pull and rpm response; $945.99 kit w/o ECU
- intake: Twin Air PowerFlow kit — Airflow; found: Better throttle response/bottom end
- exhaust: FMF 4.1 slip-on w/ spark arrestor insert — Power/weight; found: More mid, little more top; >1 lb lighter
- seat: Guts Racing Wide Top foam — Durability/ergonomics; found: Does not break down as quickly; $99.99
- controls: Works Connection Elite throttle tube — Throttle feel; found: 'buttery smooth'; $74.95
- misc: XPR Motorsports No Kinky vent hose cap — Prevent vent kink; found: $44.95

### [MXA Race Test: 2025 Yamaha YZ450F (test-bike mods)](https://motocrossactionmag.com/mxa-race-test-2025-yamaha-yz450f) — Yamaha YZ450F 2025, Motocross Action 2024-11-05

- chassis: Luxon Gen3 Pro clamps 23 mm — Calm steering; found: All testers felt it calmed steering
- intake: Twin Air PowerFlow kit — Rev/pull; found: Slightly faster rev, more mid pull; no backfire screen
- clutch: Yamaha GYTR Hydraulic clutch kit — Modulation; found: Broader modulation
- controls: Works Connection Pro Launch — Starts; found: Set 130 mm (160 mm on grates)
- seat: Guts Racing Seat (traditional profile) — Comfort; found: Preferred over stock
- drivetrain: (any) 48T rear sprocket — Taller 2nd; found: 2nd covers 2nd+3rd, less bark
- cooling: (any) 1.8 kg/cm2 radiator cap — Boil-over margin; found: Recommended over stock 1.1

### [2025 Yamaha YZ450FX long-term off-road setup](https://dirtbiketest.com/bike-tests/2025-yamaha-yz450fx/) — Yamaha YZ450FX 2025, Dirt Bike Test 2025-09

- engine mount: Yamaha OEM Remove FX front mount extra plate; YZ450F shorter bolts x2 (187155035925) — Compliance; found: Less harsh on decel bumps/rocks, slightly less lean traction
- electrical: Yamaha OEM (WR450F) Stator + flywheel (BNK-81410-02-00, BNK-81450-00-00) — Run lights; found: Powered ~75 W Baja Designs XL80
- drivetrain: (any) 14/52 or 14/53 gearing — Usable 2nd/3rd; found: Smoother delivery, better over chop
- brakes: (unspecified) Steel-braided front brake line — Lever feel; found: Recommended
- map: GYTR Power Tuner Power scale 2 (map1)/3 (map2), 'JL GH' map for vets — Tame tight terrain; found: Less jerky at slow speed

### [Yamaha YZ250F Project: Behind The Build](https://dirtbikemagazine.com/amp/yamaha-yz250f-project-behind-the-build-2/) — Yamaha YZ250F 2021, Dirt Bike 2021-10-21

- engine: Race Tech Epoxy port job — Restore bottom end lost on 2021; found: Bottom-end gains 'noticed right away', stronger mid, top less noticeable
- ecu: Vortex Ignition with custom mapping — Mapping; found: Engine died if any throttle applied while starting - start with throttle closed
- engine: Vertex Top end — Engine build
- engine: Hot Cams Stage 2 cams — Engine build
- clutch: Hinson Clutch — Durability
- exhaust: FMF Titanium 4.1 — Power
- suspension: Race Tech Gold Valve internals, seals/bushings — Handling; found: Plusher, less fatigue; soft for faster riders
- chassis: FCP Racing Titanium/aluminum engine mount kit — Front traction; found: Improved front-end traction and bite in corners (with suspension)
- intake: Slater Skins High-flow airbox lid — Airflow

## Gaps and work not done

- (yamaha-research.json) Platform boundaries confirmed from sources: YZ450F 2023 (new chassis/engine), 2025 (linkage, solid 8 mm front mounts, 56 N/mm shock), 2026 (new downtube, 4/6 mm mounts, new shock with tool-less LSC, hydraulic clutch, resonator exhaust, 13/51, password ECU); YZ250F 2024 (new frame/subframe/airbox), 2025 (linkage, 54 N/mm shock), 2027 (all-new engine, YZ450F-derived frame, new shock, hydraulic clutch); YZ450FX 2024 (2023 YZ450F platform), 2027 (new engine/frame, hydraulic clutch, 51T); YZ250FX 2025 (new gen); YZ125 2022 (new engine; YZ125X followed 2023 per Pro Circuit/FMF fitment).
- (yamaha-research.json) No maker-confirmed aftermarket exhaust fitment seen for 2026 YZ450F (Pro Circuit lists 2023-2025) or any 2027 YZ250F/YZ450FX.
- (yamaha-research.json) No maker part numbers captured for HGS, Arrow, Hinson (site needs Year/Make/Model selector), Vertex, JE, Pro-X, Hot Cams, GET, Factory Connection, Race Tech, Technical Touch, Ride Engineering, Luxon, AXP, Enduro Engineering, TM Designworks.
- (yamaha-research.json) Rekluse Core Manual TorqDrive year splits for YZ250F (RMS-7171 vs RMS-7107002) and YZ250 (RMS-7170) not shown on category page.
- (yamaha-research.json) Rekluse TorqDrive pack RMS-2807176 fitment tab lists YZ250F alongside 450s - possibly an error on the maker page.
- (yamaha-research.json) FMF 4-stroke items only confirmed via maker year filters (YZ450F 2023, YZ250F 2024), not full year ranges.
- (yamaha-research.json) Yamaha Power Tuner map values (fuel/ignition tables) in MXA and Keefer articles are images or downloads, not captured.
- (yamaha-research.json) Works Connection Pro Launch height guidance (90-110 mm in WC instructions) conflicts with MXA's 130/160 mm on YZ450F; measurement reference unknown.
- (yamaha-research.json) Little 2020-2027 data on YZ250X and YZ125X setup (only Keefer 2024 intro impressions; Dirt Bike YZ250X test found was a 2017 bike and excluded).
- (yamaha-research.json) No Australian (Transmoto, Dirt Action, ADB) Yamaha 2020+ setup data captured.
- (bars_clamps.json) Renthal 932 Fatbar36 page says OE bend on Husqvarna (2016>) and GasGas (2020>) but names no models; Husqvarna/GasGas bar fitment not recorded.
- (bars_clamps.json) Renthal 2021 recommendations give bends per model without years for Fatbar36 (933/934/930/931) and Twinwall (996/997/998/999); only Fatbar 827 recorded with year 2021, low confidence.
- (bars_clamps.json) Honda CRF250RX/450RX and Kawasaki KX250X/450X not named for the 839 OE bend; Yamaha X/FX/WR and Triumph TF models not covered by any Renthal page seen.
- (bars_clamps.json) Renthal CL061 bar mount lists 'GasGas 2022' without models; 2023+ fitment for most bar mounts (other than CL063) not printed on the listing.
- (bars_clamps.json) Renthal 798 7/8" KTM SX85 and 826/822 'KTM High/Low' bends have no years; 604 renthal.com fitment text predates roster years.
- (bars_clamps.json) No 2027 models covered: open-ended '+' ranges capped at 2026.
- (bars_clamps2.json) Handlebars (ProTaper, Neken) have no per-bike or year fitment on the maker pages. ProTaper's 'Check Fit' selector needs JavaScript and could not be read, so the bar products have empty fits. Bend names such as 'Husqvarna Stock', 'Factory Suzuki/KTM Stock', 'Original YZF', 'Original RMZ' and '250/450 CRF KXF' name a model family with no years.
- (bars_clamps2.json) ProTaper VDS 022217: the title says only 'KTM 125-500/ Gas Gas/ Husqvarna' for 2023-26. Only the KTM SX models are listed in fits; KTM XC/XC-W, the GasGas MC/EX/EC and Husqvarna TC/FC/FX/TE models of 2023-26 are not itemized by the maker.
- (bars_clamps2.json) ProTaper VDS 022218: the title 'Honda CRF450R/ 04-24 CRF450R' repeats a model and may mean CRF250R 2004-24; the page does not say. VDS 022215 Yamaha shows conflicting years (21-27 heading vs 14-26 title). VDS 022216 Kawasaki covers only up to 2024 and does not name the KX250X/KX450X.
- (bars_clamps2.json) ProTaper Fuzion: the product page lists no clamp size or colours. Sport bars: no dimensions for the track-named bends. Bend chart: no pullback column on any family.
- (bars_clamps2.json) Neken triple clamps (Standard, SFS air, SFS Spring): the maker site lists fitment only up to 2019 (e.g. SFS Spring KTM SX-SXF 2013-2019 22 mm 0509060, CRF 250/450 2018/2019 0309027, YZF 250/450 2018/2019 0409043). No 2020+ roster fitment is published, so no clamp products were recorded. SFS Spring: 10 mm travel, 22 mm offset, 28.6 mm bar clamp, 7075-T6.
- (bars_clamps2.json) Neken's site appears frozen at about 2019 (its catalogue link is the 2019 Parts Europe pageflip). Current Neken 2020+ fitment probably lives in the Parts Europe catalogue, which needs JavaScript.
- (bars_clamps2.json) TAG Metals: the moto line seems to be wound down. TAG's live site (tagmtb.com) sells only MTB parts, and US retailers (BTO Sports) mark TAG moto bars and mounts 'discontinued'. Retailer listings (motostorm.it) show T1/T2/T3 bars and 'Hon/Kaw 1 1/8 100/200/300 Bar Mounts' with no part numbers or years. Nothing recorded.
- (bars_clamps2.json) Applied Racing (moto triple clamps): the Chapmoto R/S Mount set listing (SKU 08-6362SSR family, offsets 18/20/21/21.5/22/24 mm, Silver/Red/Blue/Black) shows fitment only up to 2014. No 2020+ fitment found. Nothing recorded.
- (brakes_controls.json) Galfer's own application PDF (galfer.eu -> galfer.com/moto/.../din_a-4_kits_280mm.pdf) redirected and then the fetch permission timed out, so no Galfer maker-page fitment was captured; Galfer kits are dealer-sourced.
- (brakes_controls.json) Rocky Mountain ATV/MC pages (ProTaper EVO, ODI V2 No-Waffle) returned Incapsula bot blocks.
- (brakes_controls.json) PartsEurope oversize kit pages (17040546/17040547) need JavaScript; no fitment or part numbers loaded.
- (brakes_controls.json) EBC's own site gives no part numbers on the MXS page, and its Part Finder was not checked; EBC fitment is from J&P Cycles, with lists truncated (KTM/Husqvarna TC for MXS181, Yamaha/Suzuki for MXS185).
- (brakes_controls.json) No in-scope Galfer or Braking oversize kit with a confirmed per-bike list for Yamaha or Kawasaki; Happy Trails Kawasaki kit page gave no brand, part number or fitment; Ride Engineering Yamaha 270 kit page (aomc.mx) was 'item not found'.
- (brakes_controls.json) Nissin aftermarket upgrade parts: none found in the search budget (Nissin appears only as OEM).
- (brakes_controls.json) ODI Podium grips: not found in search results; only V2 Rogue (Cycle Gear) and Emig Pro V2 (RevZilla page with no part numbers) surfaced.
- (brakes_controls.json) ProTaper EVO and Fuzion US prices not captured; Fuzion price is UK only.
- (brakes_controls.json) Ride Engineering skid plates: not found; search returned Works Connection and Acerbis instead.
- (brakes_controls.json) Cycra handguard part numbers and bike-specific mount kits not captured.
- (brakes_controls.json) Moto-Master KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas Flame kit (RevZilla) showed no part number or fitment, so it was not added.
- (brakes_controls.json) Triumph TF coverage is thin: only the Enduro Engineering TF 250-X skid plate was confirmed; no brake, lever or bar items found for Triumph.
- (chains_wheels.json) Talon: no 2022+ KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas or 2022+ Honda sprocket fitment published (TR130RL/TG328/TR113RL/TG320GL lists stop at 2021); Honda CRF250R 2020+ not listed for any Talon sprocket.
- (chains_wheels.json) Talon: Triumph TF 250/450, Husqvarna TC 150/TC 300 (2025), KTM 300 SX, KTM 450 XCF-W, Yamaha X/FX two-strokes and YZ450FX, Kawasaki KX450X have no Talon fitment found.
- (chains_wheels.json) Talon hubs: individual rear Pro-Billet/carbon hub pages for KTM (rear carbon-centred hub), Honda (TW801A/TW753A), Yamaha (TW650A/TW731A), Kawasaki (TW928A) and KTM 85 SX junior hubs/wheels are indexed by search but return 404 on talon-eng.co.uk; hub colour names are not printed on any hub page (only 'variety of colours').
- (chains_wheels.json) Talon hub kits 602KIT/616KIT/627KIT/642KIT (bearings+spacers) have no bike fitment on their pages.
- (chains_wheels.json) Regina: maker application list (Fitment-list.pdf) has only open-ended pre-2020 ranges for MX bikes (e.g. KTM SX-F 450 '14-', Yamaha WR 450 F '13-' 114 links, Honda CRF 450RX '17-'); no explicit 2020-2027 lengths, so no fits recorded.
- (chains_wheels.json) Iris: maker site lists RX and RXP motocross chains with colour only; no pitch, lengths, codes or bike applications. The German 'IRIS 520 XR G&B' (gold & black) chains seen on shops are road chains with no maker fitment page found.
- (chains_wheels.json) Talon USA site (talonengineeringusa.com) has newer per-bike sprocket/hub pages (e.g. Honda Groovelite front, KTM Radialite rear incl. Stark Varg) but is robots-blocked for fetching.
- (chassis.json) Luxon MX, Neken, Tag Metals, Applied, Emig, Scotts triple clamps: no 2020+ product page reached.
- (chassis.json) Ride Engineering engine mounts, linkages, holeshot devices; Pro Circuit, Moose and Tag holeshot devices: not found.
- (chassis.json) GPR and Tusk steering dampers: no 2020+ MX fitment page reached.
- (chassis.json) OEM optional engine mounts (HRC, GYTR, Kawasaki, KTM): not found, beyond the YZ450FX tester mod.
- (chassis.json) ProTaper, Neken, Tag bars; ODI Podium mounts; ARC, Works Connection and Motion Pro levers/perches; IMS and Pro Circuit pegs: not captured.
- (chassis.json) Galfer rotor kits (KG349 found with no fitment), Brembo, Nissin/Brembo master-cylinder conversions, Galfer/Brembo/SBS pads: no usable fitment.
- (chassis.json) Excel A60, DID Dirt Star, Talon and Haan wheels sold directly: only bundled listings found.
- (chassis.json) TM Designworks, Moose, Polisport protection; Cycra/Acerbis handguards: no 2020+ fitment captured.
- (chassis.json) Selle Dalla Valle and Throttle Syndicate seats/covers: no 2020+ listings found.
- (chassis.json) Triumph TF 450-X/RC, TF 250-C/TF 450-C: only holeshot and skid-plate fitment found.
- (chassis.json) Few 2026-2027 fitments confirmed; most lists end at 2024-2026.
- (chassis2.json) Smart Performance (Factory Spec, valving kits, revalve service): no relevant search hits for the company or products
- (chassis2.json) Cannon Racecraft (revalve, lowering): no relevant search hits
- (chassis2.json) Factory Connection revalve service pricing and per-bike shock spring PNs: not found; only Cycle Gear fork-spring listing loaded (RevZilla listings had no PNs)
- (chassis2.json) Ride Engineering per-bike part numbers: ride-engineering.com not in search results (fetch blocked); Rocky Mountain ATV/MC pages blocked by bot protection; AOMC Ride pages return Item Not Found
- (chassis2.json) Ride Engineering link arms for 2020+ Honda/Yamaha/KTM, engine mounts: not found (only 2013-14 CRF450 linkage system, out of scope)
- (chassis2.json) Race Tech fork/shock springs for 2020+ bikes: only generic FRSP listings without fitment found
- (chassis2.json) Tag Metals triple clamps and holeshot device: no listings found
- (chassis2.json) Neken clamps: only 2013-2017 KTM listings (out of scope years)
- (chassis2.json) Applied Racing clamps: only 2007-era CRF article found
- (chassis2.json) Emig bars/clamps: no hits
- (chassis2.json) Tusk steering dampers: no hits (Tusk sells steering stem bearings only in results)
- (chassis2.json) Moose Racing holeshot device: no hits
- (chassis2.json) MotoSport product pages for KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas Factory Start and Pro Circuit Launch Control: fetch permission not granted
- (chassis2.json) Pro Circuit Launch Control per-bike fitment not found
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Mishimoto: the MX catalogue (mishimoto.com /fits/ and the MX/ATV hose and radiator pages) shows only dirt-bike radiators and hose kits up to about 2014 (e.g. CRF450R 2002-2008, YZ450F 2010-2013, YZ125 2005-2014). No 2020+ roster bike was found.
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Fluidyne: fluidyne.com lists only car and race-car radiators. Fluidyne Powersports MX radiators appear only in 2009-2011 press items. No current dirt-bike line or 2020+ fitment was found.
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) CV4: no maker site was found. Dealer pages (Cycle Gear, RevZilla, JJ Motorsports) show CV4 hose kits only up to about 2016 for MX bikes (plus CRF150R) and print no colour or part-number variants. No 2020+ roster fitment, so no CV4 silicone hose colours could be recorded.
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Psychic: appears to be an Australian brand sold by Firestorm Motorcycles. The only listings found were older radiators (e.g. WR250 2015-2018, CRF450R 2002-2008). No 2020+ roster radiator or hose kit was found.
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Enduro Engineering: enduroeng.com lists radiator brace part numbers 11-017 to 11-8018 and guard part numbers 12-1116 to 12-9025 (12-1116BK; the 3/16 in guards are black anodised), but prints no make/model/year per part number. Dealers say 12-1116 fits '2016-2024 KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas' (Walmart title only).
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Web Cams (webcamshafts.com): grinds are listed for KTM 450 SXF DOHC (62-011, 62-121, 62-021), KTM 250 SXF DOHC (62-082 / 62-072), CRF450R SOHC (59-912, 59-982, 59-992, 67-232) and CRF250R SOHC (57-092, 57-522, 57-312, 67-242). Spring kits VC-K41 (Kibblewhite) and VC-K52 are listed for the KTM 450 SXF SOHC. No model years are printed, so these are not recorded as fits.
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Hot Cams / Vertex: no Hot Cams camshaft was found for any 2023+ roster four-stroke. Newest found: CRF450R 2017-2020 Stage 1/2 (dealer title only), KTM-group 250 to 2021/22, YZ450F 2018-2019. ProX YZ450F '23 exhaust valve 28.2453-1 exists, but its page and the matching intake could not be opened.
- (drivetrain.json) JT catalogue pages for Husqvarna TC 125/TC 150/TC 250/TC 300/FC 250/FC 350/FX 450 (2025) and Yamaha YZ250X (2026) show part numbers 'TBA' - no JT fitment recorded for those model-years.
- (drivetrain.json) JT catalogue has no listings found for KTM 125 SX/150 SX/etc. 2027, Husqvarna 2026-2027 MX models, GasGas EX 250 (2022-23, 2025), EX 250F/350F/450F 2025, MC 350F 2025, Yamaha YZ125/YZ250 2027 at time of check.
- (drivetrain.json) Renthal front sprocket group 492U-520 listed in Renthal application data but not found in renthal.com store.
- (drivetrain.json) Husqvarna FC 250 2024-2026: Renthal's OE-size front field shows 291--415-18P (415 pitch) while its front group is 292--520 - the front group is recorded with a note
- (drivetrain.json) KTM 85 SX 19/16 2020: Renthal chain SKU C272 (oe_chain) not found in Renthal's MX/off-road chain store listings
- (drivetrain.json) KTM 85 SX 19/16 2021: Renthal chain SKU C272 (oe_chain) not found in Renthal's MX/off-road chain store listings
- (drivetrain.json) KTM 85 SX 19/16 2022-2027: Renthal chain SKU C272 (oe_chain) not found in Renthal's MX/off-road chain store listings
- (drivetrain.json) Renthal Fit My Bike data has no records found for: KTM 125 SX 2025-2027, Husqvarna TC 125 2025-2026, Husqvarna FC 450 2027, Husqvarna FX 350 2026-2027, Husqvarna FX 450 2027, Triumph TF 250-C / TF 450-C / TF 450-X 2026, and TF 250-E / TF 450-E 2026 (Renthal lists only 2025 E models).
- (drivetrain.json) Renthal Fit My Bike data contains internal inconsistencies (e.g. CRF250R 2022-2024 rear listed 49T while JT lists 50T; KX450X 2023-2024 oe_chain C413 = R3-3 114L vs r1 C128 = 120L) - recorded as stated.
- (drivetrain.json) Supersprox application tables show no in-scope rows for KTM 350 SX-F, KTM 300 SX, KTM 125 XC, Husqvarna TC 300, GasGas MC 250/MC 250F/MC 350F and EX range, Kawasaki KX250X/KX450X, Yamaha YZ125X (Stealth) - not recorded. Triumph 'TF 250 X Enduro'/'TF 450 X Enduro' rows on RST-990 were not mapped (ambiguous vs TF 250-E / TF 450-X).
- (drivetrain.json) Supersprox 428-pitch patterns RFE-1134 and CST-277 list '125 SX 2018-2026' with no make; not recorded (modern KTM 125 SX is 520).
- (drivetrain.json) Sunstar: maker sprocket catalogue no longer online (sunstar-braking.com redirects to sunstar-mc.com, an OEM fine-blanking/stamping site with no products). Retailers checked: Rocky Mountain ATV/MC (bot wall), MotoSport (only 3 clearance variants, no fitment in page), Speed Addicts (fitment loads by script, not readable), PureMoto (opaque fitment codes). Only RevZilla closeout pages recorded; no Sunstar steel rear, front sprockets or Sunstar chains recorded.
- (drivetrain.json) Vortex: vortexracing.com returns 403; Vortex data is RevZilla-only. Vortex front sprockets and KTM/Kawasaki CAT5 aluminium pages not located.
- (drivetrain.json) Rocky Mountain ATV/MC, Cycle Gear, Partzilla and Dennis Kirk blocked automated access (Incapsula/403) - no Tusk or Primary Drive kit data.
- (drivetrain.json) RK: rkchain.com did not load; RK sprocket kits not researched.
- (drivetrain.json) Renthal chain/sprocket prices are renthal.com UK store (GBP); US pricing not captured. JT site shows no prices.
- (drivetrain.json) Supersprox prices are supersprox.com (EUR) and many variants show out of stock.
